Abstract: Audio-visual cross-modal Fine-Grained Visual Categorization (FGVC) aims to identify fine-grained categories by jointly leveraging visual and auditory information. However, FGVC under asymmetric cross-modal scenarios has received limited attention, where paired video and audio are not strictly synchronized and may not even correspond to the same individual or moment. Such weak and ambiguous cross-modal correspondence poses substantial challenges to effective representation learning and modality alignment. To address these issues, we propose ACF-Net, a novel optical flow-guided framework for asymmetric audio-visual fine-grained learning. ACF-Net consists of two key modules: Optical Flow-Guided Motion (OFGM) and Asymmetric CrossModal Adaptive Fusion (ACAF). OFGM captures motion-sensitive visual cues and suppresses irrelevant background interference, thereby enhancing discriminative dynamic representations in videos. ACAF estimates modality reliability under weakly matched audio-video pairs and performs uncertainty-aware adaptive fusion to improve category-level recognition robustness. To support research on asymmetric cross-modal FGVC, we further construct BirdPro, a new bird-oriented audio-visual benchmark, since existing datasets often lack large-scale category-level audio-video associations under non-strict temporal and instance correspondence. BirdPro contains 1,919 audio recordings and 11,965 videos covering 194 bird species. Extensive experiments show that ACF-Net achieves the best results compared with representative baseline methods, outperforming the strongest baselines by 2.97% and 1.92% in the fused and mismatched settings, respectively.

</details>


### [41] [Video-IFBench: Evaluating Instruction Following of Multimodal LLMs in Video Understanding Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25529)
*Hongbo Liu,Peixian Chen,Sihan Liu,Peiyuan Zhang,Kai Zou,Dian Zheng,Xiaoxing Hu,Yuhao Dong,Mengdan Zhang,Yunhang Shen,Haoyu Cao,Wei Liu,Weibo Gu,Xing Sun,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Video-IFBench，一个用于评估视频理解中多模态大模型指令遵循能力的综合基准，包含4种指令模板、32种任务类型和39种约束类别，共1.5K样本，并评估了20多个最新的多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频理解基准主要关注任务准确性而非指令遵循能力，而真实世界的视频理解需要模型满足多样的用户指定约束，这一能力尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 构建了包含四类指令模板（单任务、多任务、选择、嵌套）的指令分类体系，覆盖32种任务类型和39种人工设计的约束类别（包括语义和格式要求）；采用半自动数据构建流程，结合MLLM、程序化处理和人工验证，最终生成1.5K样本。

Result: 对20多个最新的MLLM进行了大规模评估，发现视频指令遵循对当前模型仍具挑战性，尤其涉及多约束指令、语义约束以及需要根据视频内容选择正确分支或路径的复杂条件结构时表现更差。

Conclusion: 视频理解中的指令遵循仍是当前MLLM的重要短板，该基准有望推动未来该方向的研究。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong performance in video understanding. However, their ability to follow instructions in this domain remains under-explored. Real-world video understanding requires models not only to interpret video content correctly, but also to satisfy diverse user-specified constraints. Existing benchmarks focus primarily on task accuracy rather than instruction adherence, leaving this capability insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce Video-IFBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating instruction following in video understanding, where models must satisfy diverse user-specified constraints, including those grounded in visual and audio content. We develop an instruction taxonomy with four templates, including single-task, multi-task, selection, and nested instructions, covering 32 task types and 39 manually designed constraint categories spanning both semantic and format requirements. To reduce annotation cost, we build a semi-automatic data construction pipeline that combines MLLMs, programmatic processing, and human verification, resulting in 1.5K samples. We conduct a large-scale evaluation of more than 20 recent MLLMs and show that video instruction following remains challenging for current models, especially for instructions with many constraints, semantic constraints, or complex conditional structures that require selecting the correct branch or path based on video content. We hope our work will facilitate future research on instruction following in video understanding scenarios.

</details>


### [42] [CropCop: An Auditable 120-Class Plant-Health Model from Benchmark Reconstruction to a Quantised Runtime Artifact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25539)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CropCop，一个覆盖120类植物健康状态的闭集识别系统。通过严格的泄漏控制（剔除3,233个跨分割边界的重复关系）、冻结109,107张图像的基准，并利用DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny微调达到98.51%准确率；最终通过量化与ExecuTorch/XNNPACK转换，生成22.60 MiB的PTE文件，实际执行时保持98.46%准确率，验证了从数据集构建到运行时部署的全链路可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有植物健康评分可能因重复图像族、长尾标签分布或未实际评估的运行文件而产生虚假的精确性。作者旨在构建一个从数据采集到最终运行时工件全链路都有严格证据链的封闭集识别系统，避免数据泄漏和评估失真。

Method: (1) 从117,546张审计图像出发，识别并剔除3,233个跨分割边界的重复关系，冻结109,107张图像的无交叉测试基准。(2) 全量微调DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny作为参考模型。(3) 训练MobileNetV4 Conv-Medium紧凑模型。(4) 验证集-only后训练量化，选择动态激活与逐通道权重，转换生成ExecuTorch/XNNPACK PTE工件，并直接执行最终量化文件进行真实评估。(5) 对INT8图与PTE之间的16,363个top-1决策进行逐样本对比分析。

Result: 参考模型（DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny）达到98.51%准确率和96.87% macro-F1；紧凑MobileNetV4 Conv-Medium达到98.46%准确率和96.27% macro-F1；最终22.60 MiB的PTE文件直接执行时保持98.46%准确率和96.23% macro-F1。INT8图与PTE之间仅有6/16,363个top-1决策不同。事后水果标签切片分析发现比聚合准确率更大的召回率下降。

Conclusion: CropCop建立了严格的泄漏控制的内部识别性能和软件运行时保真度，但未声称在未见农场、相机管道或实体Android硬件上的泛化性能。研究强调从数据重建到最终工件执行的全链路证据对模型评估可信度的重要性。

Abstract: A plant-health score can appear precise while resting on duplicated image families, a long-tailed label space, or a runtime file that was never evaluated. We present CropCop, a closed-set recognition system spanning 120 operational plant-health classes and an evidence chain from corpus reconstruction to direct execution of the final quantised artifact. Starting from 117,546 audited images, we rejected the inherited partition after confirming 3,233 duplicate relationships across split boundaries and froze a 109,107-image benchmark with zero crossings among the audited trusted leakage groups and a 151.7 largest-to-smallest class ratio. A fully fine-tuned DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny reference achieved 98.51% accuracy and 96.87% macro-F1 on the locked internal test. A compact MobileNetV4 Conv-Medium derivative achieved 98.46% accuracy and 96.27% macro-F1 without being presented as evidence for a new distillation method. Validation-only post-training quantisation selected dynamic activations with per-channel weights, and the final 22.60 MiB ExecuTorch/XNNPACK PTE achieved 98.46% accuracy and 96.23% macro-F1 when executed directly. Only six of 16,363 top-1 decisions changed between the converted INT8 graph and the PTE, while paired analysis showed a modest class-balanced loss; an exploratory post hoc fruit-label slice localized a larger recall decline than aggregate accuracy revealed. CropCop establishes strong leakage-controlled internal recognition and software-runtime fidelity; it does not establish performance on unseen farms, camera pipelines, or physical Android hardware.

</details>


### [43] [AdaVDR: Adaptive Tool Use and Reflection for Video Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25559)
*Xintong Zhang,Xiaomeng Fan,Shilin Yan,Ekko He,Zicheng Liu,Zijian Zou,Guannan Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Zhi Gao,Hongwei Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaVDR，一个自适应视频深度研究智能体，通过自适应工具调用和反思机制，根据任务需求动态选择工具并仅在必要时回溯，结合数据构建管线和强化学习训练，在VDR-EE基准上达到开源模型最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频深度研究需要联合理解视频内容和从开放网络检索外部知识，但不同问题和视频需要不同的工具使用策略，不恰当的工具调用会产生错误结果，不确定的定位和检索也会导致不必要的交互成本、延迟和推理错误。

Method: AdaVDR包含三个关键组件：(1) 自适应工具调用与反思——根据任务和自身能力选择工具，仅在中间结果不可靠时回溯；(2) 视频深度研究数据构建管线——通过事件和实体发现、定位和外部检索构建高质量QA对，生成任务特定的工具使用轨迹；(3) 模型条件化工具必要性过滤——评估目标模型的视频理解和内部知识能力，移除可被跳过的工具或工具链。训练采用监督微调加冗余感知奖励的强化学习。

Result: 在VDR-EE基准（覆盖实体中心和事件中心问题）上，AdaVDR在开源模型中表现最佳，并显著超越其基础模型在VideoDR上的表现。

Conclusion: 通过自适应工具调用策略和针对目标模型能力的轨迹定制，可以有效提升视频深度研究的准确性和效率，减少不必要的工具交互成本。

Abstract: Video deep research answers complex questions by jointly understanding video content and retrieving external knowledge from the open Web. However, diverse questions and videos require different tool-use strategies, and inappropriate tool calls can produce incorrect results. Uncertain grounding and retrieval also make unnecessary interactions costly and error-prone, increasing latency and reasoning errors. To address these challenges, we propose AdaVDR, an adaptive video deep research agent with adaptive tool invocation and reflection. AdaVDR selects tools according to the task and its capabilities, and backtracks only when unreliable intermediate results require correction. To enable these capabilities, we develop a video deep research data construction pipeline. We first discover retrieval-relevant events and entities in diverse videos and acquire detailed information through grounding and external retrieval to construct high-quality QA pairs. For each QA, task-specific prompts organize the information acquisition process into a tool-use trajectory, allowing different question and video types to follow different grounding and retrieval strategies. We further introduce model-conditioned tool necessity filtering, which evaluates tool calls against the target model's video understanding and internal knowledge, removing tools or tool chains the model can bypass. This yields trajectories tailored to the target model's video understanding capability and knowledge. Using this pipeline, we construct training data and VDR-EE, a benchmark covering entity-centric and event-centric questions. We perform supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with a redundancy-aware reward to strengthen adaptive tool invocation and reflection. Experiments show that our method performs best among the evaluated open-source models on VDR-EE and substantially improves over its base models on VideoDR.

</details>


### [44] [CrossMambaTuning: Synergistic Spatial and Cross-Layer Adaptation for Machine Vision Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25568)
*Haobo Xiong,Shaobo Liu,Kai Liu,Chongyang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossMambaTuning框架，将状态空间模型（Mamba）与跨层交互机制结合，用于参数高效的预训练图像压缩模型微调，在多个机器视觉任务上达到SOTA性能，同时将参数开销减少72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常独立地将微调模块插入冻结骨干网络中，缺乏跨层协调的显式机制，限制了模型在下游机器视觉任务上的适应能力。

Method: 提出CrossMambaTuning框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 高效的Mamba适配器，配备任务特定提示和多尺度分支，以捕获局部特征和全局依赖；(2) 尺度不变的跨层适配器（SICA），利用参数共享策略融合不同尺度的任务信息并减少冗余。

Result: 在多个机器视觉任务上达到SOTA性能，相较SOTA方法参数开销降低72%。

Conclusion: CrossMambaTuning通过跨层交互机制和Mamba架构，有效解决了预训练图像压缩模型适配下游任务时缺乏跨层协调的问题，实现了性能提升与参数效率的兼顾。

Abstract: To reduce deployment cost and retraining overhead, adapting pretrained learned image compression (LIC) models to downstream machine vision tasks has attracted growing attention. However, existing methods typically insert fine-tuning modules independently into frozen backbones, lacking explicit mechanisms for cross-layer coordination. To address this limitation, we propose a novel framework named CrossMambaTuning, which integrates State Space Models with cross-layer interaction mechanisms for parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Specifically, we design an efficient Mamba adapter equipped with task-specific prompts and multi-scale branching to precisely capture both local features and global dependencies. Furthermore, we introduce a Scale-Invariant Cross-Layer Adapter (SICA) utilizing a parameter-sharing strategy to fuse task information across different scales and reduce redundancy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CrossMambaTuning achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on multiple machine vision tasks, reducing parameter overhead by 72\% compared to SOTA methods. Code is available at https://github.com/rsr1123/CrossMambaTuning.

</details>


### [45] [MLLMCLIP: Feature-Level Distillation of MLLM for Robust Vision-Language Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25575)
*Jongsuk Kim,Qiyu Wu,Zhuoyuan Mao,Hiromi Wakaki,Junmo Kim,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MLLMCLIP，一种异构蒸馏框架，直接从生成式多模态大语言模型（MLLM）教师蒸馏多模态知识到判别式CLIP学生，绕过合成数据生成流水线，通过注意力逐层token选择和CKA蒸馏损失解决架构不匹配问题，在组合推理、零样本分类和图文检索上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型（如CLIP）在零样本识别上表现出色，但在组合性（属性-物体和关系结构）上经常失败。现有方法通过级联的大语言模型和文本到图像模型生成合成硬负例来缓解该问题，但带来了巨大的流水线开销。作者希望绕过合成数据生成，直接进行特征级蒸馏。

Method: 提出MLLMCLIP异构蒸馏框架，将一个生成式MLLM教师的多模态知识转移到判别式CLIP学生中。为弥合两种范式间的架构不匹配，引入基于注意力的逐层token选择机制和基于CKA的蒸馏损失函数。

Result: 相比先前的CLIP增强方法，MLLMCLIP在组合准确率上达到最先进水平，同时在标准零样本分类和图文检索任务上带来持续提升，表明特征级蒸馏能够同时增强组合性和通用视觉语言表征能力。

Conclusion: 特征级蒸馏从生成式MLLM到判别式CLIP的范式是可行的，且能同时提升组合性理解和通用视觉语言表征能力，无需依赖昂贵的合成数据生成流程。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models such as CLIP excel at zero-shot recognition but often fail at compositionality, particularly attribute-object and relational structures. Recent studies mitigate this issue by augmenting training with synthetic hard negatives generated by a cascade of large language models and text-to-image models, which incurs substantial pipeline overhead. We instead propose MLLMCLIP, a heterogeneous distillation framework that transfers multimodal knowledge directly from a generative Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) teacher into a discriminative CLIP student, bypassing synthetic data entirely. To bridge the architectural mismatch between the two paradigms, we introduce an attention-based per-layer token selection and a CKA-based distillation loss. Compared to prior CLIP-enhancement methods, MLLMCLIP achieves state-of-the-art compositional accuracy while delivering consistent gains on standard zero-shot classification and image-text retrieval, showing that feature-level distillation strengthens both compositional and general vision-language representation capability.

</details>


### [46] [V-Rubrics: Visual Faithfulness via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25580)
*Shulin Tian,Minglun Li,Yuhao Dong,Hao Ding,Jiarui Yao,Haiwen Diao,Jingkang Yang,Hongyuan Zhu,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于视觉评分标准的强化学习（Visual Rubrics-Based Reinforcement Learning），将参考答案分解为原子命题，并从视觉忠实度、推理一致性和指令遵循三个维度对生成答案进行打分，以解决多模态后训练中标量奖励无法定位信用分配失败的问题。在Qwen3-VL-8B基础上训练，实验表明基于评分标准的GRPO优于共享SFT基线和仅答案奖励的GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型生成的答案虽然流畅但可能缺乏充分的视觉证据支撑，单个未支撑的物体、图表值或中间推理步骤就能破坏整体回答。作者认为这是多模态后训练中的信用分配（credit-assignment）失败——标量结果奖励只能告知答案是否可接受，却无法指出哪些视觉事实已接地、哪些推理步骤有效、哪些指令约束被遗漏。

Method: 1) 将参考答案分解为原子命题，从视觉忠实度（VF）、推理一致性（RC）和指令遵循（IF）三个维度评分生成的答案；2) 通过微调Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct获得SFT检查点；3) 构建V-Rubrics 50K数据集（50,248个示例），来源于17个视觉接地数据源，使用规则过滤、拒绝采样评分和Gemini-3-Pro标注；4) 使用基于组件、前缀局部化的评分标准信用进行训练。

Result: 实验表明基于评分标准的GRPO在共享SFT基线和仅答案奖励的GRPO上均有提升，最大的提升出现在知识导向和视觉接地推理基准上。

Conclusion: 评分标准是一种有用的视觉后训练奖励抽象，能够提供结构化部分信用并定位信用分配，有效改善多模态模型输出的视觉接地性。

Abstract: Vision-language models can produce fluent answers that are insufficiently grounded in the visual evidence: a single unsupported object, chart value, or intermediate inference can undermine an otherwise plausible response. We argue that this is a credit-assignment failure in multimodal post-training. Scalar outcome rewards indicate whether an answer is acceptable, but do not identify which visual facts are grounded, which reasoning steps are valid, or which instruction constraints are missed. We introduce Visual Rubrics-Based Reinforcement Learning, which decomposes reference responses into atomic propositions and scores generated answers along Visual Faithfulness (VF), Reasoning Consistency (RC), and Instruction Following (IF). The resulting rubric items provide structured partial credit and localize rubric credit when supporting evidence spans are available. We first obtain an SFT checkpoint by fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct on the public OpenMMReasoner-SFT-874K corpus, adapting OpenMMReasoner's cold-start data recipe. We construct V-Rubrics 50K, a 50,248-example training set from 17 visually grounded sources, by applying rule-based filters before deriving example difficulty from rejection-sampling scores and then annotating every example with Gemini-3-Pro under the same structured prompt and protocol. We train our model based on the same SFT checkpoint using component-wise, prefix-localized rubric credit. Experiments show that our rubricbased GRPO improves over both the shared SFT baseline and answer-only GRPO, with the largest gains on knowledge-oriented and visually grounded reasoning benchmarks. The results show rubrics as a useful reward abstraction for visual post-training.

</details>

*Josep Cabacas-Maso,Carles Ventura,Ismael Benito-Altamirano*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双Transformer和交叉注意力的多摄像机自动编辑模型，在TVMCE数据集上以56.60%的Precision@0.5大幅超越现有最优方法（37.16%），并证明了仅用20%目标视频微调即可实现编辑风格个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 所提出的Dual-Transformer架构在多摄像机编辑任务上显著优于现有方法，且具备数据高效的编辑风格个性化能力，有望适应不同电视节目或制片人的编辑风格。

Abstract: Multi-camera systems are foundational to modern media production, and multi-camera editing is a critical task. This involves the proper selection of the appropriate camera view at each moment. In this paper, we propose a novel Dual-Transformer architecture with Cross-Attention that heavily outperformed the current SOTA models over the TVMCE dataset (TV Shows Multicamera Editing dataset). Our model decouples these tasks: (1) a dedicated temporal encoder first processes the sequence of past frames to build a rich memory of the recent history, and (2) the candidate camera views then act as queries to this memory via a cross-attention module, allowing each candidate to independently interrogate the historical context and find the most relevant information for its own evaluation.
  Our approach achieved 56.60% Precision@0.5, representing a substantial improvement over the prior best result of 37.16%. We further conducted an ablation study exploring the use of lightweight backbone architectures, where the SwinV2 backbone yielded the best performance, achieving 69.65% Precision@0.5.
  Using this best-performing configuration, we then investigated the feasibility of adapting the model to replicate the editing style of a specific human editor. To this end, we fine-tuned the model using varying proportions of the initial segment of a target video. Our results demonstrate that even with only 20% of the video used for fine-tuning, the model exhibited measurable improvements in Precision@0.5, indicating strong potential for data-efficient personalization of editing style adapted to each individual TV show or producer.

</details>


### [48] [When Should a Network Emit Geometry, and When Should It Detect It? Readout, Reconciliation, and Representation in Floorplan Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25608)
*He Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文比较了栅格化平面图恢复网络两种输出方式：自回归坐标序列生成与稠密热图检测+图组装。在真实扫描数据上检测方式更优，但在干净矢量渲染图上序列解码更好。两种方式融合可提升效果，而相互条件化无增益。论文还提供了编辑代价指标、修正标注和新基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对相同训练网络采用不同输出表示（序列解码vs热图检测）的优劣缺乏系统对比，且结果受数据分布影响，需要明确在何种条件下哪种输出表示更优。

Method: 在同一训练网络上对比两种输出方式：1)自回归坐标序列生成几何；2)在稠密junction和centerline热图上检测并组装图。在多个数据集（CubiCasa5K真实扫描、干净矢量渲染图）上评估，并进行跨域迁移测试、输出融合实验、输入条件化实验，同时提出编辑代价评估指标。

Result: 真实扫描上热图检测在墙指标上全面优于序列解码；但在干净矢量渲染图上序列解码在训练覆盖风格下好5-8点。输出融合可提升墙F1达7点，而条件化输入无增益。检测方式在读未见过的开口热图上无需重训即可提升开口F1 2.6倍。

Conclusion: 输出表示的选择不如通常假设的重要——合理配置下两种方式可达相当质量，但最优方式取决于数据域；输出级融合有效而输入级条件化无效。论文提供了新基准ResPlan-FP和修正后的CubiCasa5K标注。

Abstract: A network trained to recover the walls, openings, and rooms of a rasterized floorplan can produce its output in two ways: by emitting the geometry as an autoregressive coordinate sequence, or by detecting it on dense junction and centerline heatmaps and assembling a graph. We compare the two readouts on the same trained network. On real scans (CubiCasa5K) detection is better on every wall measure (+2.7 wall F1 at tolerance 0.05, +5.1 at 0.015; paired bootstrap intervals exclude zero), and reading an opening heatmap the decoder never used raises opening F1 by 2.6x without retraining. Within real scans the readout's advantage grows with plan size and reverses on small plans; on clean vector renders sequence decoding is better by 5 to 8 points where its training covered the render style, while under full domain shift the readout, given calibrated thresholds, stays ahead; neither ink density nor plan size explains the reversal. With matched data and recipe, a room-centric system with a reconciliation step and a wall-first sequence model reach comparable wall quality, so the output representation matters less than is usually assumed. A prior from the other family helps at the output but not at the input: deterministic fusion of the two outputs raises wall F1 by 7 points, whereas conditioning one model on the other's output gives no gain in three forms, including two ground-truth-content controls. We also provide an edit-cost metric that scores a draft by the human work needed to correct it, corrected CubiCasa5K annotations, and ResPlan-FP, a CC BY 4.0 benchmark of 16,998 plans with frozen splits and three baseline tracks. Code, the benchmark, and the corrected annotations are available at https://github.com/Cyprinus12138/fpvec-lab

</details>


### [49] [On the Separation of Human and AI-Generated Images in CLIP Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25609)
*Andrea Asperti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究发现在CLIP表征中，人类与AI生成的绘画会沿联合嵌入分布的主方向自发分离（无需监督），并通过可解释图像表征与梯度反演系统探究这一现象背后的视觉信息，揭示该分离源于多尺度分布式图像结构，且对低感知显著性扰动高度敏感，反映了CLIP视觉证据与人类感知之间的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解释而非利用CLIP嵌入中人类与AI绘画自发分离的现象，试图识别造成这种分离的视觉信息，并探究人工视觉与人类视觉在美学判断上的关系差异。

Method: 采用渐进式调查方法：结合可解释图像表征（如多尺度散射变换等）与梯度反演技术，系统性地作为实验探针，通过鲁棒性实验和日益精细的统计描述符逐步排除基于全局图像属性和简单局部统计的假设。

Result: 实验排除了多种基于全局属性和简单局部统计的解释，指向分布式多尺度图像结构；多尺度散射是最具信息量的可解释表征但仅能部分解释该现象。直接反演显示沿主方向的显著位移可由近似不可察觉的图像扰动诱发，表明该分离对低感知显著性变化高度敏感。

Conclusion: CLIP表征所反映的视觉证据与人类可感知的视觉信息存在显著差异，这引发了关于人工与人类视觉关系以及美学判断差异的更广泛思考。

Abstract: We identify a previously unreported phenomenon in CLIP representations: human and AI-generated paintings spontaneously separate along the dominant principal directions of their joint embedding distribution, without any supervised objective designed to distinguish the two classes. Rather than exploiting this phenomenon for detection, our objective is to interpret it: we seek to identify the visual information underlying the separation and to trace it back from the embedding space to the image domain. We pursue this objective through a progressive investigation combining interpretable image representations with gradient-based inversion, used systematically as an experimental probe of the relationships identified in feature space. Robustness experiments and increasingly expressive statistical descriptors progressively rule out several intuitive explanations based on global image properties and simple local statistics, and point instead to distributed multiscale image structure. Multiscale scattering provides the most informative interpretable representation considered, but offers only a partial account of the phenomenon.
  Direct inversion provides a complementary and striking observation: substantial displacements along the dominant CLIP directions can be induced by image perturbations that remain nearly imperceptible to human observers, showing that the directions involved in the separation are highly sensitive to image variations with very low perceptual salience for humans. Taken together, these results reveal a significant difference between the visual evidence reflected in CLIP representations and that readily accessible to human perception, raising broader questions about the relationship between artificial and human vision and, ultimately, between artificial and human aesthetic judgment.

</details>


### [50] [Plans You Can Check: Verifier-Grounded Learning of an Open-Weight Planner for Executable Video-Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25622)
*Haoyu Wang,Cheng Feng,Liuyang Bian,Ruiyang Huang,Lei Wei,Yafei Wen,Xiaoxin Chen,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RefineCut，训练一个紧凑的开源规划器用于可执行视频编辑规划，通过验证器重放的蒸馏方法和进化式偏好优化，使8B参数的规划器在闭环验证器中超越其前沿教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用的视频编辑不仅仅是像素生成，编辑者需要将需求、素材片段、音乐元数据和硬约束转化为可执行的剪辑时间线。现有工作流系统通常包装提示式前沿模型，但缺乏针对这一决策层的专门训练。

Method: RefineCut通过结构化补丁编辑带类型的剪辑时间线（涵盖片段选择、裁剪、排序、转场、时长和音乐对齐）；确定性验证器对每个补丁应用并对照显式约束台账检查。不直接模仿教师，而是让多个教师分支通过验证器重放，保留验证器最优解作为监督信号。第二阶段RefineCut-Evo让学生用验证器和任务评分标准为自己的修复打分，对高边际偏好对训练，最终8B规划器在推理时闭环运行验证器，无需教师调用。

Result: 在RefineCut-Bench（3,578个任务、7,971个带字幕片段、499首音乐、显式约束台账）上，验证器重放蒸馏将Video-Editing Score从0.620提升到0.858，RefineCut-Evo达到0.924；增益可迁移至Llama-3.1-8B和GLM-4-9B，8B规划器在闭环中达到或超过前沿教师模型。

Conclusion: 可执行视频编辑规划应作为独立决策层进行专门训练，通过验证器驱动的蒸馏和偏好优化，紧凑开源模型可以在闭环中媲美甚至超越前沿教师模型，并提供公开代码与基准数据集。

Abstract: Practical video editing is not only pixel generation: an editor must turn a brief, a clip pool, music metadata, and hard constraints into an executable timeline. We study this decision layer as \emph{executable video-editing planning} and introduce RefineCut, which, unlike workflow systems that wrap a prompted frontier model, trains a compact open-weight planner for it. The planner edits a typed timeline through structured patches covering clip selection, trimming, ordering, transitions, and duration and music alignment; a deterministic verifier applies each patch and checks it against an explicit constraint ledger. Because editing has no single ground-truth repair, we do not imitate teachers directly: RefineCut replays every multi-teacher branch through the verifier and keeps verifier-best repairs as supervision. A second stage, RefineCut-Evo, lets the student score its own repairs with the verifier and a task rubric and trains on high-margin preference pairs, so the final $8$B planner runs in a closed verifier loop with no teacher calls at inference. On RefineCut-Bench ($3{,}578$ tasks, $7{,}971$ captioned clips, $499$ music tracks, explicit ledgers), verifier-replayed distillation lifts the planner from $0.620$ to $0.858$ on the protocol-specific Video-Editing Score and RefineCut-Evo reaches $0.924$; the gain transfers to Llama-3.1-8B and GLM-4-9B, and in the same closed loop the $8$B planner matches or exceeds its frontier teachers. Code and RefineCut-Bench are publicly released; see the Data Availability statement.

</details>


### [51] [SeVeR: Selective Visual Exposure and Retrieval for 3D Medical Image Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25630)
*Yaojun Hu,Danyang Tu,Yang Liu,Jiajin Zhang,Wei Fang,Zhiqiang Liu,Chunlai Dong,Yingda Xia,Haochao Ying,Jian Wu,Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了BreMRIs-VQA，一个包含119万对QA的乳腺MRI医学视觉问答基准，并提出了SeVeR框架，通过选择性视觉暴露机制压缩密集3D视觉序列、检索互补多模态证据，在减少视觉token数量的同时提升判别和生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多序列MRI中3D视觉token序列冗长且存在大量重复解剖区域，增加了推理负担。现有医学视觉问答方法未充分处理多序列视觉冗余问题，缺乏专门针对乳腺MRI的VQA基准。

Method: 1) 构建BreMRIs-VQA临床乳腺MRI基准（71.0K序列、12.9K患者、1.19M QA对）；2) 提出SeVeR框架：将密集体量压缩为模态级原型，解码时通过变化感知门控注意力检索互补多级证据；3) 采用边际效用自一致性训练目标抑制无益检索。

Result: 在BreMRIs-VQA及公开基准上，SeVeR在判别和生成任务上均取得更优性能，同时暴露显著更少的视觉token。

Conclusion: SeVeR证明了选择性视觉暴露策略能有效处理多序列MRI的冗余问题，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少视觉token暴露，为体积医学VQA提供了高效推理新范式。

Abstract: Volumetric medical VQA requires reasoning over long and redundant 3D visual token sequences, especially in multi-sequence MRI where complementary modalities provide diverse diagnostic cues but expose the decoder to many repeated anatomical regions. To investigate reasoning under multi-sequence visual redundancy, we first introduce BreMRIs-VQA, a clinically curated breast MRI benchmark with 1.19M QA pairs from 71.0K sequences and 12.9K patients, covering both free-text and multiple-choice questions. We further propose SeVeR, a selective visual exposure framework that compresses dense volumes into modality-wise prototypes and retrieves complementary multi-level evidence with change-aware gated attention during decoding, trained with a marginal-utility self-consistency objective that suppresses unhelpful retrieval. Experiments on BreMRIs-VQA and public benchmarks show that SeVeR improves both discriminative and generative performance while exposing substantially fewer visual tokens.

</details>


### [52] [MAMA-FLUX.2: Image-to-Image Synthesis of Post-Contrast Breast DCE-MRI for the MAMA-SYNTH Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25648)
*Kamil Kwarciak,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAMA-FLUX.2，一种基于FLUX.2-Klein-4B的条件潜在流匹配方法，用于乳腺MRI增强前后图像合成。通过LoRA微调、全局流匹配与肿瘤区域监督相结合的损失函数，实现了无需对比剂即可合成增强后MRI图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态对比增强乳腺MRI对癌症诊断至关重要，但依赖钆基对比剂，存在安全性风险。本文旨在通过合成增强后MRI图像来减少或消除对对比剂的依赖。

Method: 基于FLUX.2-Klein-4B预训练模型，将增强前图像作为空间条件编码，预测增强后目标潜变量的流场。采用LoRA参数高效微调，并设计区域训练目标，结合全局流匹配、肿瘤区域监督和前景稳定正则化。

Result: 通过消融实验研究了LoRA秩、强度窗口化和区域损失权重的影响。最优配置为LoRA秩/α=64/64、MHA_max=25、λ_tumor=0.25、λ_stable=0.1，在图像保真度和肿瘤区域准确性之间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 紧凑的预训练整流流Transformer可通过参数高效微调和任务感知的区域损失有效适应对比增强MRI合成任务，证明了该技术路线的可行性。

Abstract: Dynamic contrast-enhanced breast MRI is central to cancer diagnosis and monitoring, but requires gadolinium-based contrast agents. In this work, we address pre-to-post contrast breast MRI synthesis for the MAMA-SYNTH challenge. We propose MAMA-FLUX.2, a conditional latent flow-matching approach based on FLUX.2-Klein-4B. The pre-contrast image is encoded as spatial conditioning, while the model predicts the flow field associated with the post-contrast target latent. To adapt the pretrained model efficiently, we use LoRA fine-tuning and introduce a regional training objective combining global flow matching, tumor-region supervision, and stable foreground regularization. We further investigate LoRA rank, intensity windowing, and regional loss weights on axial slices, prioritizing clinically relevant tumor-focused metrics. Our ablation study shows that moderate tumor and stable-foreground weighting improves the trade-off between image fidelity and tumor-region accuracy. The final model achieves the best overall balance with LoRA rank/$α=64/64$, $\mathrm{MHA}_{\max}=25$, $λ_{\mathrm{tumor}}=0.25$, and $λ_{\mathrm{stable}}=0.1$. These results demonstrate that compact pretrained rectified-flow transformers can be adapted for contrast-enhanced MRI synthesis using parameter-efficient fine-tuning and task-aware regional losses.

</details>


### [53] [Diffusion Transformers for Roof Graph Synthesis and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25652)
*Daniel Panangian,Ksenia Bittner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoofDiT是一个用于二维屋顶图合成与重建的生成框架，采用两阶段设计：扩散Transformer生成屋顶顶点，边缘预测模块推断图拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有屋顶结构重建方法依赖固定几何规则或直接重建目标，缺乏对屋顶图结构（顶点和边）的生成式建模能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 扩散Transformer生成屋顶顶点；(2) 边缘预测模块推断图拓扑。结合相对几何感知注意力、足迹和航拍图像条件化，以及对齐正则化来鼓励水平、垂直和对角线屋顶模式。通过改变条件信号支持无条件生成、足迹条件合成和图像引导重建。

Result: 在扩散基线上图生成质量提升；在足迹条件下优于直线骨架先验方法；在图像引导重建中取得最高的边缘F1分数。

Conclusion: RoofDiT展示了将屋顶结构建模为顶点-边图并学习条件生成先验的有效性，统一了多种生成和重建任务的框架。

Abstract: We present RoofDiT, a generative framework for 2D roof graph synthesis and reconstruction. Roofs are compactly described as planar graphs of junctions and structural edges, but existing methods often rely on fixed geometric rules or direct reconstruction objectives. RoofDiT instead models roof structures directly as vertex-edge graphs and learns a conditional generative prior over their geometry and connectivity. Our framework follows a two-stage design: a diffusion transformer generates roof vertices, and an edge prediction module infers the corresponding graph topology. To improve geometric fidelity, RoofDiT combines relative geometry-aware attention with footprint and aerial-image conditioning, while using an alignment regularizer to encourage common horizontal, vertical, and diagonal roof patterns. The same model supports unconditional generation, footprint-conditioned synthesis, and image-guided reconstruction by changing the conditioning signal. Experiments show improved graph generation quality over a diffusion baseline, favorable performance against a straight-skeleton prior in the footprint-conditioned setting, and the highest edge F1 among compared methods for image-guided reconstruction.

</details>


### [54] [Towards Purified Multi-Label Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25653)
*Yiwen Liang,Hui Chen,Yizhe Xiong,Mengyao Lyu,Yuhan Cao,Zijia Lin,Shuaicheng Niu,Sicheng Zhao,Jungong Han,Guiguang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PuRF是一种基于纯化驱动的缓存方法，用于视觉语言模型的多标签测试时适应（MLTTA），通过区域纯化和缓存纯化解决多标签场景下共享全局表征导致的 dominant-label 偏差和缓存校准问题，在五个数据集上平均提升4.05% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时适应（TTA）方法主要针对单标签识别，而现实图像常包含多个物体。直接扩展基于缓存的方法到多标签场景会面临一对多映射问题：共享全局表征纠缠了共现物体，导致主导标签偏差和缓存校准受损。

Method: PuRF包含两个关键步骤：(1) 区域纯化——识别可靠区域，提供全面的区域线索用于多标签识别和细粒度对齐；(2) 缓存纯化——基于纯化后的区域，通过片段式纯化构建有区分度的基于区域的缓存，并通过时间刷新提升长期缓存适应性。

Result: PuRF在五个数据集上一致优于现有最先进方法，使用ViT-B/32时平均mAP提升4.05%。

Conclusion: PuRF通过区域纯化和缓存纯化有效解决了多标签测试时适应中的一对多映射问题，提升了视觉语言模型在多标签场景下的分布偏移鲁棒性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has been widely explored in single-label recognition, effectively mitigating distribution shifts, especially when combined with vision-language models. However, real-world images often contain multiple objects, while the more practical multi-label test-time adaptation (MLTTA) has received little attention so far. Recent cache-based TTA methods have shown promising efficiency and effectiveness, yet directly extending them to multi-label scenarios suffers from a one-to-many mapping problem: a shared global representation entangling co-occurring objects is stored as class-wise cache prototypes, inducing dominant-label bias and compromised cache calibration. While introducing region-level cues helps isolate class-specific evidence, such regional evidence can also be unreliable under distribution shifts, making its identification and utilization non-trivial. To address these issues, we introduce PuRF, a novel PuRiFication-driven cache-based method for multi-label test-time adaptation of vision-language models. Specifically, PuRF first performs region purification to identify reliable regions, providing comprehensive regional cues for multi-label recognition and enabling fine-grained alignment. Based on these purified regions, PuRF conducts cache purification to enhance cache representation and adaptability, where episodic purification builds a discriminative region-based cache, and temporal refreshing further promotes long-term cache adaptability. Experiments demonstrate that PuRF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving a notable 4.05% mAP improvement on ViT-B/32 across five datasets.

</details>


### [55] [Deep Learning Segmentation of Diffusion-Weighted MRI Acute Ischaemic Stroke: A Pragmatic Evaluation Across Three Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25675)
*Atle Bjørnerud,Till Schellhorn,Thor H. Skattør,Terje Nome,Jon André Ottesen,Anne Hege Aamodt,Bradley J MacIntosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了使用nnU-Net深度学习模型进行急性缺血性卒中DWI病灶分割的实用性，发现基线nnU-Net配合仅DWI输入、无需预处理即可实现快速准确的病灶分割，性能优于DeepISLES基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习已被证明可以准确分割急性缺血性卒中病灶，但最佳图像输入格式和模型架构仍不确定，且现有方法往往涉及复杂预处理。研究旨在探索一种最小预处理、临床推理时间可行的务实深度学习方法。

Method: 使用自配置nnU-Net模型，在1,744例DWI病例（来自本地、国家和开放数据集）上训练，436例测试。通过五折交叉验证评估四种实验条件（有无脑提取、DWI单独或DWI+ADC输入），比较基线nnU-Net与残差编码器nnU-Net两种架构，并与2022年ISLES挑战赛的DeepISLES集成模型进行基准对比。

Result: 基线模型在测试集中获得中位Dice相似系数0.84（IQR 0.19）。仅2/6配对比较显示输入配置间显著差异。ResEnc在DWI、DWI+脑提取、DWI+ADC输入下较基线仅产生小幅但显著的DSC提升（p<0.02），但在DWI+ADC+脑提取条件下无显著差异。基线模型显著优于DeepISLES，尤其在小梗死体积患者中表现突出。

Conclusion: 基线nnU-Net仅使用DWI图像、无需预处理即能实现快速准确的AIS病灶分割，这种简化方法可能促进临床研究并支持急性卒中影像工作流程。

Abstract: Objective: Diffusion-weighted MRI (DWI-MRI) is the gold standard for visualizing and quantifying acute ischaemic stroke (AIS). Although deep learning methods can accurately segment AIS lesions, the optimal image inputs and model architecture remain uncertain. We evaluated whether accurate AIS lesion segmentation can be achieved using a pragmatic deep learning approach with minimal preprocessing and clinically feasible inference times.
  Materials and Methods: Self-configured nnU-Net models were trained on 1,744 DWI cases from local, national, and open-access datasets and tested on 436 cases. Four experimental conditions were evaluated using five-fold cross-validation: with or without brain extraction and using either DWI alone or DWI plus apparent diffusion coefficient (ADC) images as inputs. Two architectures were compared: the baseline nnU-Net (base) and a residual encoder nnU-Net (ResEnc). Performance was benchmarked against the DeepISLES ensemble model from the 2022 ISLES challenge.
  Results: In the test set (n=436), the base model achieved a median (IQR) Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) of 0.84 (0.19). For the base model, only two of six pairwise comparisons between input configurations showed significant differences. ResEnc produced small but significant improvements in DSC compared with the base model for DWI, DWI+brain extraction, and DWI+ADC inputs (all p<0.02), but not for DWI+ADC+brain extraction (p>0.50). The base model significantly outperformed DeepISLES, particularly in patients with smaller infarct volumes (signed-rank test, p<0.01).
  Conclusions: A baseline nnU-Net trained on DWI alone, without preprocessing, enabled fast and accurate AIS lesion segmentation. This streamlined approach may facilitate clinical research and support acute stroke imaging workflows

</details>


### [56] [CloSeR: Unified Relational Distillation from Closed-Set Teachers for Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25692)
*Yuanpei Liu,Zhenqi He,Jialu Tang,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CloSeR框架，通过注入闭集关系知识到广义类别发现（GCD）训练中，利用块级适配器构建闭集教师模型，并通过统一关系蒸馏（URD）转移知识，以解决闭集识别与开集发现之间的目标冲突问题，在六个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法将监督分类和无监督发现目标联合优化，导致闭集识别与开集发现相互纠缠，产生目标冲突和偏差预测；且在有限标签和噪声伪标签条件下，可能破坏预训练表征的语义几何结构。

Method: CloSeR框架包含两个关键组件：1）闭集教师构建——在冻结基础模型骨干的情况下，仅微调轻量级块级适配器于标注已知类数据上，保留预训练先验并降低训练成本；2）统一关系蒸馏（URD）——通过分离的特征通路，蒸馏全局样本到原型的关系以锚定已知类语义，以及局部样本到样本的关系以保留邻域结构，减少优化干扰。该框架与具体头部无关，可集成到参数化和非参数化GCD方法中。

Result: 在DINO和DINOv2骨干网络下，于六个基准数据集（CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet-100、CUB、Stanford-Cars、FGVC-Aircraft）上进行大量实验，相比GCD基线方法取得一致的性能提升，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: CloSeR通过注入闭集关系知识有效缓解了GCD训练中的目标冲突问题，是一种简单、即插即用且与头部无关的通用框架，能够显著提升现有GCD方法的性能。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) is an intriguing open-world problem that has garnered increasing attention: given partially labelled data, the goal is to correctly recognize known classes while discovering coherent novel categories from unlabelled samples. Recent GCD methods typically adapt foundation models by jointly optimizing supervised classification and unsupervised discovery objectives on mixed labelled and unlabelled data. While effective, this coupled training can entangle closed-set recognition and open-set discovery, leading to objective conflict and biased predictions, and may disturb the semantic geometry of pretrained representations under limited labels and noisy pseudo-labels. We propose CloSeR, a simple plug-and-play framework that injects Closed-Set Relational knowledge into GCD training. CloSeR first builds a domain-adapted closed-set teacher by tuning lightweight block-wise adapters on labelled known-class data while keeping the foundation model backbone frozen, thereby preserving pretrained priors at low training cost. It then transfers the teacher's knowledge to downstream GCD via Unified Relational Distillation (URD), which distills complementary global sample-to-prototype relations to anchor known-class semantics and local sample-to-sample relations to preserve neighborhood structure, using separate feature pathways to reduce optimization interference. CloSeR is head-agnostic and readily integrates with both parametric and non-parametric GCD methods. Extensive experiments with DINO and DINOv2 backbones on six benchmarks (CIFAR-10/100, ImageNet-100, CUB, Stanford-Cars, and FGVC-Aircraft) show consistent gains over GCD baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/closer/

</details>


### [57] [Unsupervised Anatomical Feature Learning via Diffusion Models: Enhanced Medical Image Segmentation with Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25693)
*Akshat G,Divyansh Gupta,Shaleen Bhatnagar,Shilpa Ankalaki,Tusar Kanti Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用无监督去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）在未标注的腹部CT图像上预训练，提取解剖学特征，并将编码器权重迁移到下游医学图像分割任务中，显著提升U-Net在低数据场景下的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割需要昂贵的像素级标注，传统U-Net仅学习局部纹理模式，缺乏全局解剖结构感知，在低数据条件下容易产生边界分割失败。

Method: 在21个未标注的腹部CT扫描上训练DDPM以学习结构表示，将编码器权重迁移到BTCV多器官数据集上的下游分割任务中，并评估冻结与微调两种模式下的性能。

Result: 扩散预训练显著提升性能：肝脏Dice从0.75提升至0.93，ASD降低66%，HD95降低45%；肾脏Dice从0.90提升至0.95；多器官分割方差减少68%，边界精度提升74%。冻结编码器保留超过80%的微调性能，且在仅10%标注数据时仍保持稳健表现。

Conclusion: 利用未标注图像进行扩散预训练可有效嵌入解剖学先验知识，将U-Net转变为具备解剖结构感知能力的系统，减少对大量人工标注的依赖。

Abstract: Acquiring pixel-level annotations for medical image segmentation is a severe bottleneck. Traditional U-Net architectures, while effective, learn local texture patterns and lack awareness of global anatomical structures, leading to boundary delineation failures in low-data regimes. This research paper proposes utilizing unsupervised Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPMs) to extract anatomical features. We train a DDPM on 21 unlabeled abdominal CT scans to learn structural representations, transferring the encoder weights to a downstream segmentation task evaluated on the BTCV multi-organ dataset. Diffusion pretraining significantly improved liver segmentation: Dice increased from $0.75\pm0.36$ to $0.93\pm0.16$ ($p < 5.33\times10^{-26}$, 0.529 Cohen's d), Average Surface Distance (ASD) decreased by 66%, and 95th-percentile Hausdorff Distance (HD95) reduced by 45%. For kidney segmentation, Dice improved from $0.90\pm0.19$ to $0.95\pm0.10$ ($p < 4.01\times10^{-11}$). Multi-organ pooled performance showed a 68% variance reduction and a 74% improvement in boundary precision (Dice $0.95\pm0.07$). Crucially, frozen encoder models retained > 80% of fine-tuned performance without exposure to segmentation labels, proving the existence of learned anatomical priors. In low-data scenarios, diffusion-pretrained models maintained robust performance with only 50% (Dice: 0.92 liver, 0.94 kidney), 25%, and even 10% (Dice: 0.89 liver, 0.71 kidney) of labeled data. Using unlabeled images for diffusion-based pretraining successfully embeds robust anatomical features prior to human supervision, transforming U-Nets into anatomy-aware systems.

</details>


### [58] [Skeleton-based Zero-Shot Spatio-Temporal Action Localization via Weakly-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25701)
*Koshiro Nagano,Fumiaki Sato,Ryo Hachiuma,Kazuki Tsutsukawa,Taiki Sekii*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于骨架的零样本时空动作定位预训练策略，通过弱监督视觉-语言预训练机制（Skeleton-Language feature Pooling Switching）和场景混合判别对比学习（Scene-Mixed Discriminative Contrastive Learning）来解决动作标注成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时空动作定位方法依赖大量人工标注，成本高昂；同时现有方法难以泛化到未见过的动作类别。

Method: 提出Skeleton-Language feature Pooling Switching机制，实现从预训练阶段（视频级骨架特征聚合与动作文本嵌入对齐）到推理阶段（无训练的目标动作实例级特征计算）的池化核转换；并提出Scene-Mixed Discriminative Contrastive Learning，通过MIL框架在场景内区分实例级动作。

Result: 在四个公开的时空动作定位和分类数据集上验证了方法的有效性，能够解决标注不足的问题。

Conclusion: 该方法通过提出新的预训练策略有效解决了骨架零样本时空动作定位中的标注成本高和未见动作泛化问题。

Abstract: We propose a novel pretraining strategy for skeleton-based zero-shot spatio-temporal action localization to estimate unseen actions for person instances while overcoming high annotation costs for training via new target actions and pretraining using large-scale action scenery datasets. Specifically, our approach, termed Skeleton-Language feature Pooling Switching, introduces a weakly-supervised vision-language pretraining mechanism. This mechanism transitions pooling kernels from pretraining, which aggregates skeleton features at the video level and aligns them with each video's known action text embeddings, to the inference phase that computes instance-level features without training via target actions. Furthermore, we propose Scene-Mixed Discriminative Contrastive Learning to distinguish actions at the instance level within the combined scene through the MIL framework. Our experiments on four public spatio-temporal action localization and classification datasets demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses annotation limitations.

</details>


### [59] [Difficulty-Aware Sample Allocation for Adaptive Data Augmentation in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25710)
*Olasimbo Ayodeji Arigbabu,Abimbola Ismail Arigbabu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DASA（难度感知样本分配）框架，将预测模糊度、训练损失、类别稀有度和边界复杂度等多维度难度信号融合为归一化难度分数，并据此为困难样本分配更强的数据增强。在Oxford-IIIT Pet和Pascal VOC分割任务上，DASA方法优于标准训练，并与其他自适应增强基线相当或更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强技术在语义分割中要么均匀分配变换，要么仅基于单一困难信号（如损失），忽视了分割难度是多因素交织的：模糊预测、持续优化错误、稀有类别和复杂边界都会使样本在不同维度上具有信息量。

Method: DASA整合四类难度信号（预测模糊度、训练损失、类别稀有度、边界复杂度），计算归一化难度分数，并将该分数映射到逐样本的增强强度，在迭代训练过程中动态调整。

Result: 在Oxford-IIIT Pet和二元Pascal VOC分割任务上，使用U-Net、DeepLabV3和SegFormer-B0进行实验。DASA在Oxford-IIIT Pet上将DeepLabV3的mIoU从0.633提升至0.740；在二元Pascal VOC上，DASA在三种架构上均取得最佳前景IoU。

Conclusion: 多因素难度估计是引导增强资源定向分配的实用有效机制，值得作为语义分割中增强策略的设计原则。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a standard component of modern semantic segmentation pipelines, but most augmentation techniques allocate transformations uniformly across training samples or adapt to a single difficulty signal such as loss. This ignores the fact that segmentation difficulty is multi-factorial, since ambiguous predictions, persistent optimization errors, rare classes, and complex object boundaries can each make a sample informative in different ways. This paper introduces Difficulty-Aware Sample Allocation (DASA), an architecture-agnostic framework that assigns stronger augmentation to samples estimated to be more difficult. DASA combines prediction ambiguity, training loss, class rarity, and boundary complexity into a normalized difficulty score, then maps that score to sample-specific augmentation strength during iterative training. Experiments on Oxford-IIIT Pet and binary Pascal VOC segmentation with U-Net, DeepLabV3, and SegFormer-B0 show that DASA improves over standard training and is competitive with or stronger than single-signal adaptive baselines. On Oxford-IIIT Pet, DASA improves DeepLabV3 from 0.633 to 0.740 mIoU. On binary Pascal VOC, DASA obtains the best foreground IoU for all three evaluated architectures. These results attest to the value of multi-factor difficulty estimation as a practical mechanism for directing augmentation where it is most useful.

</details>


### [60] [LongVU-TTT: Causal Test-Time Training for Visual Resampling in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25729)
*Mahmoud Ahmed,Sameh Abdulah,Olatunji Ruwase,Sam Ade Jacobs,Mathis Bode,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongVU-TTT提出了一种基于卷积测试时训练（TTT）重采样器的长视频多模态大语言模型方法，通过因果快速权重更新在视觉编码器与LLM之间实现时间上下文化，并采用均匀与变化感知混合选择器保留视觉证据，在多个视频理解基准上取得竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频MLLM面临视觉token预算有限的问题，在压缩前需要建模时间变化，但现有方法要么依赖注意力机制（计算成本高），要么使用固定状态的循环重采样器（表达能力有限），难以在有限的视觉token预算内有效保留时序信息。

Method: 在视觉编码器与LLM之间插入卷积测试时训练（TTT）重采样器，采用因果快速权重更新，使用分组2D快速权重逐视频自适应并上下文化帧特征后再压缩；同时设计混合均匀与变化感知的选择器，显式保留视觉证据供下游推理。

Result: 在受控条件下，TTT-Conv相比TTT-MLP在MLVU上最高提升+2.12，相比双向Mamba2最高提升+3.04；在三个基准上优于注意力和固定状态循环重采样器。LongVU-TTT可处理多达512帧后缩减至128个LLM帧，在五个视频理解基准上取得竞争力表现。

Conclusion: 快速权重表现为时间聚合状态而非可靠的长时程情景记忆，其收益随证据距离增大而衰减，这验证了显式帧保留策略的必要性，也为长视频MLLM的token预算分配提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Long-video MLLMs must model temporal change before a limited visual-token budget removes most frame evidence. We introduce LongVU-TTT, which inserts a convolutional Test-Time Training (TTT) resampler with causal fast-weight updates between the vision encoder and the LLM. Its grouped 2D fast weights adapt to each video and contextualize frame features before compression, while a hybrid uniform-and-change-aware selector retains explicit visual evidence for downstream reasoning. Under controlled conditions, TTT-Conv improves over TTT-MLP by up to +2.12 and bidirectional Mamba2 by up to +3.04 on MLVU, and it is stronger than attention- and fixed-state recurrent resamplers across three benchmarks. Analysis shows that the fast weights behave as a temporal aggregation state rather than a reliable long-horizon episodic memory: their benefit attenuates as evidence becomes more distant, motivating explicit frame retention. LongVU-TTT processes up to 512 frames before reducing them to 128 LLM frames and achieves competitive performance across five video understanding benchmarks.

</details>


### [61] [MIMONet: Multi-scale Input and Multi-scale Output Network for Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25733)
*Zhaojian Yao,Wei Gao,Tiesong Zhao,Hui Yuan,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出MIMONet，一种多尺度输入与多尺度输出的显著性检测网络，通过三分支图像金字塔结构学习目标尺寸变化知识，结合多尺度感知模块和联合显著性损失，在多个数据集上取得更优检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性检测方法虽采用多级特征融合，但输入为单一尺寸图像，难以学习显著目标尺寸变化的知识，导致对多尺度目标的检测能力不足。

Method: 提出MIMONet：1) 使用三种不同分辨率的图像构建三个编码器分支并在分支间交换信息，使特征学习到目标尺寸变化知识；2) 设计多尺度感知(MSP)模块，将输入特征层划分为多个不同分辨率的子层以充分感知目标；3) 提出联合显著性损失(JSL)约束多个输出显著性图识别同一前景目标并保持边界清晰。

Result: 实验结果表明MIMONet在多个数据集上相比现有模型具有更强的检测能力和更好的评估分数。

Conclusion: 通过多尺度输入输出和跨分支信息交换，模型能有效学习目标尺寸变化知识，提升多尺度显著性检测性能。

Abstract: The existing methods for saliency detection task focus on the application of multi-level features, aiming to take advantage of the respective strengths of high- and low-level features. However, because the inputs of these models are single-size images, their multi-level features have difficulty in learning the knowledge of size variations of salient objects. Object-scale variation learning has great potential for detecting multi-scale objects, which has not been fully explored by existing methods. To improve the recognition ability of a model for objects with different sizes, we are inspired by the image pyramid to propose a Multi-scale Input and Multi-scale Output Network (MIMONet). In MIMONet, we extract multi-level features for three images with different resolutions to form three encoder branches, and information will be exchanged between the branches. The advantage of this approach is that the features of one branch can learn the knowledge of target size variation from the features of the other two branches. In addition, we design a Multi-scale Perception (MSP) module, in which the input feature layer is divided into several sub-layers with different resolutions. Capturing the multi-level structure information of the objects in these sub-layers can make the objects more fully perceived. For network training, we propose a Joint Saliency Loss (JSL), which can constrain multiple saliency maps output by the network to identify the same foreground objects, and induce their boundaries to be preserved clearly. Experimental results show that MIMONet has stronger detection capabilities and harvests better evaluation scores on multiple datasets compared to existing models. The code of our model will be released.

</details>


### [62] [InteractGesture: Progressive Chunk Guidance for Continuous Streaming Co-Speech Gesture Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25734)
*Ekkasit Pinyoanuntapong,Ajinkya Deogade,Paul Streli,Wenjing Zhang,Joanna Materzynska,Pu Wang,Vittorio Ferrari,Jie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InteractGesture，一种模型无关的推理时方法，用于共语手势生成中的细粒度空间可控性，通过可微RVQ-VAE解码器将空间控制梯度反向传播到扩散采样器的潜在估计中，并提出Progressive Chunk Guidance策略解决流式生成中的块间依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共语手势生成模型虽然能生成逼真的全身动作，但缺乏对单个关节点进行细粒度空间控制的能力，无法满足用户对特定关节位置和轨迹的精确操控需求。

Method: InteractGesture通过可微RVQ-VAE解码器将空间控制梯度反向传播到扩散采样器的目标潜在估计中，从而在采样过程中调整运动潜在量；同时提出Progressive Chunk Guidance块窗口策略，维护一组带交错延迟的可编辑块潜在量，使空间约束能够在流式生成中跨块边界向后传播梯度。

Result: 在BEAT2数据集上的实验表明，InteractGesture在多关节空间控制上取得改进，同时保持了整体手势质量；该方法还支持稀疏关节定位、密集关节轨迹控制和定向指向等多种应用。

Conclusion: InteractGesture提供了一种模型无关、推理时的空间可控手势生成新方法，通过梯度反传实现细粒度关节控制，并有效解决了流式生成中的块间一致性问题。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture generation has made significant progress toward realistic full-body motion from speaker audio, yet existing models lack fine-grained spatial controllability of individual joints. To address this, we introduce \emph{InteractGesture}, a model-agnostic, inference-time method for spatially controllable gesture generation. \emph{InteractGesture} guides target latent estimates of a diffusion sampler through a differentiable RVQ-VAE decoder, backpropagating spatial control gradients to adjust motion latents during sampling. A primary challenge in streaming co-speech generation is chunk-wise dependency: standard sequential inference freezes prior chunks, preventing spatial constraints in future chunks from adjusting preceding trajectories and causing boundary inconsistencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose \emph{Progressive Chunk Guidance}, a chunk-window strategy that maintains an active set of editable chunk latents with staggered delays, enabling spatial constraints to propagate gradients backward across chunk boundaries during streaming generation. Experiments on the BEAT2 dataset show that \emph{InteractGesture} improves multi-joint spatial control while preserving overall gesture quality. Furthermore, our approach supports diverse applications, including sparse joint positioning, dense joint trajectory control, and directional pointing. Our project page is available at https://exitudio.github.io/interactgesture-page .

</details>


### [63] [Moving Beyond More Views: Redundancy-Aware Ego-Exo Fusion for Proficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25736)
*Xu Dong,Wanqing Li,Anthony Adeyemi-Ejeye,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出AdaMVS和VIB-GB两个互补模块，解决EgoExo熟练度评估中多视角融合导致的冗余和过拟合问题，在EgoExo-4D和EgoExo-Fitness数据集上取得新的最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多外中心视角虽然提供空间上下文，但简单添加更多视角会因冗余或噪声视角稀释有用的运动线索，导致EgoExo性能下降。作者识别出两个关键原因：多视角冗余（数据层面）和过拟合（特征层面）。

Method: 提出两个互补模块：(1) AdaMVS，在数据层面通过弱监督自适应识别并融合最具信息量的视角token；(2) VIB-GB，在特征层面结合梯度混合和变分信息瓶颈正则化，压缩冗余信号并抑制训练过拟合。

Result: 在EgoExo-4D和EgoExo-Fitness数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够学习"看哪个视角"以及"如何融合"，取得了新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: 该工作深入分析了EgoExo熟练度评估中多视角融合性能退化的问题根源，并提出了数据层面和特征层面的双重解决方案，验证了自适应视角选择与信息瓶颈正则化的有效性。

Abstract: EgoExo proficiency estimation aims to assess action quality by integrating fine-grained motion cues from egocentric (1st-person) views with spatial context from multiple exocentric (3rd-person) views. Simply adding more exocentric views degrades EgoExo performance, as redundant or noisy perspectives dilute useful motion cues. Our analysis identifies two key causes: (1) Multiview redundancy - From the data perspective, certain views provide limited or noisy information, diluting discriminative cues; (2) Overfitting - From the feature perspective, conventional fusion increases representational complexity, causing the model to memorise view-specific patterns rather than learn generalisable representations. To address these issues, we propose two complementary modules: AdaMVS, which adaptively identifies and fuses the most informative view tokens under weak supervision from the data perspective, and VIB-GB, which combines Gradient Blending and Variational Information Bottleneck regularisation from the feature perspective to compress redundant signals and suppress overfitting during training. Experiments on EgoExo-4D and EgoExo-Fitness demonstrate that our method learns both which view to look at and how to fuse them, achieving new state-of-the-art results. Our source code is available at https://github.com/dx199771/AdaMVS

</details>


### [64] [TDFNet: Tri-projection Deformable Fusion Network for Panoramic Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25808)
*Qiangqiang Zhou,Jiacong Yu,Jiawei Xu,Yong Chen,Xin Huang,Ping Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDFNet，首个三投影可变形融合网络用于全景显著目标检测，通过结合等距柱状投影、立方体映射和切面投影三种互补表示，利用跨投影可变形注意力与纬度引导融合模块来缓解投影几何畸变，提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景显著目标检测在机器人视觉和虚拟现实中应用广泛，但现有基于投影的方法存在严重问题：等距柱状投影（ERP）存在极地拉伸畸变，立方体映射（CMP）在面边界处存在不连续性，导致特征判别力下降和几何一致性受损。

Method: 提出TDFNet，包含三个关键组件：（1）跨投影可变形注意力（CDA）模块，利用不同投影间的空间对应关系构建几何感知采样位置，引导可变形注意力进行跨投影上下文聚合；（2）纬度引导融合（LGF）模块，利用球面纬度先验构造几何置信权重，自适应平衡ERP和CMP特征，并结合切面投影的降畸变语义参考实现跨投影特征精炼与空间对齐；（3）三分支编码架构，同时保留全局空间连续性、局部几何细节和细粒度边界信息。

Result: 通过三投影互补表示和几何感知的可变形注意力机制，有效缓解了投影畸变问题，提升了全景显著目标检测的性能。

Conclusion: TDFNet通过多种投影互补策略解决了单一投影的固有缺陷，实现了更鲁棒和准确的全景显著目标检测。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the growing potential of panoramic salient object detection in robotic vision, virtual reality, and related applications. However, projecting spherical scenes onto 2D planes inevitably introduces geometric distortions, which fundamentally limit the effectiveness of existing projection-based methods. Specifically, Equirectangular Projection (ERP) suffers from severe polar stretching distortions, while cube map projection introduces discontinuities across cube-face boundaries, resulting in degraded feature discriminability and compromised geometric consistency. To address these limitations, we propose TDFNet, the first Tri-projection Deformable Fusion Network for panoramic salient object detection, exploiting complementary projection representations to alleviate geometric distortions and improve detection performance.Specifically, we design a cross-projection deformable attention (CDA) module that leverages spatial correspondences between different projections to construct geometry-aware sampling locations, guiding deformable attention for cross-projection contextual aggregation and enhancing robustness against projection-induced deformations. Furthermore, we introduce a latitude-guided fusion module, which utilizes spherical latitude priors to construct geometric confidence weights for adaptively balancing ERP and CMP features. Meanwhile, LGF incorporates distortion-reduced semantic references from Tangent Projection to achieve cross-projection feature refinement and spatial alignment.By constructing a three-branch encoding architecture based on ERP, CMP, and Tangent Projection, TDFNet simultaneously preserves global spatial continuity, local geometric details, and fine-grained boundary information.

</details>


### [65] [Label-Free Foundational Model Selection for Medical Image Classification under Distribution Shift via Pseudo Label Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25810)
*Juan Iñaki Larrea,Lucas Mansilla,Enzo Ferrante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AURCC，一个无需目标域标注的标签-free选择准则，用于在多个基础模型中选择最适合在未标注目标域部署的医学影像模型。该方法基于SUDO框架，在胸部X光分类的跨医院场景下，对多种视觉-语言模型进行排序，Spearman相关系数最高达0.943。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学图像分析中部署时，由于机构间分布偏移，性能差异很大，而目标域标签通常不可得。因此需要一个无标签的选择准则，来解决"有多个候选基础模型和源域标注数据时，该部署哪一个到未标注目标域"的实际问题。

Method: 基于SUDO框架构建AURCC评分：将未标注目标数据按预测概率分区，对每个区域测量反映类别污染的伪标签差异，聚合各区域得分得到AURCC。该指标无需目标域标注和微调，可用于对多个视觉-语言模型（如BioMedCLIP、CXR-CLIP、CheXzero、MedCLIP、MedImageInsight、CLIP）进行排序。

Result: 在胸部X光分类的三种跨医院分布偏移场景下，AURCC排序与真实排序的Spearman相关系数最高达0.943（p<0.05）。与基于源域保留集准确率的基线相比，当标注源域数据充足时AURCC具有竞争力，当源域数据较少时AURCC排序更准确。

Conclusion: AURCC为医学影像基础模型在未标注目标域的模型选择提供了一种有效的无标签解决方案，尤其在源域标注数据有限的资源受限场景下优势明显。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed for medical image analysis. However, under the inter-institutional distribution shift typical of deployment, their performance varies widely and cannot be known without target-domain labels, which are rarely available. This leaves a practical question unresolved: given several candidate foundational models and labeled-data from a source domain, which one to deploy in an unlabeled target domain? We propose a label-free selection criterion built on SUDO, a framework for evaluating clinical AI systems without ground-truth annotations. SUDO partitions the unlabeled target data by predicted probability and, for each region, measures a pseudo-label discrepancy reflecting class contamination; aggregated across regions, this yields a score (AURCC) requiring neither target annotation nor fine-tuning. We show that AURCC can be used to rank a variety of vision-language models (BioMedCLIP, CXR-CLIP, CheXzero, MedCLIP, MedImageInsight, CLIP) on chest X-ray classification across three inter-hospital shift scenarios, under zero-shot and MLP-probe regimes. The AURCC ranking recovers the ground-truth ranking with Spearman rho up to 0.943 (p<0.05). Against the natural baseline of ranking by held-out source accuracy, AURCC is competitive when the labeled source is large and yields a more accurate ranking once it is small; the regime of interest in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [66] [Steer the Sampling, Not the Kernel Grid: Geometry-Guided Sampling Operator for Volumetric Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25819)
*Sizhe Wang,Himashi Peiris,Zhaolin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何引导的局部算子，通过预测局部方向和有限步长来引导特征采样，替代传统卷积核变形，用于3D医学图像分割中细小管状结构（如血管）的精确分割，在多个数据集上取得显著改善并大幅减少参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长、细小的解剖/病理结构（如血管）在3D分割中极易因单个体素的边界误差导致分支断连和拓扑变化。传统的编码器-解码器网络（如U-Net）在下采样和固定网格卷积中会模糊或混叠细小结构，削弱方向线索，导致早期错误跨尺度传播。

Method: 提出几何引导的局部算子，在采样阶段而非卷积核变形阶段进行引导：(1) 每个体素预测局部方向和有界步长；(2) 沿方向对称采样；(3) 将成对样本转化为紧凑的几何和边界线索，通过轻量混合处理；(4) 跨尺度一致性机制对齐跳跃连接处的编码器和解码器特征。该算子统一支持步长为1的特征细化和步长大于1的分辨率缩减，可替换3D U-Net中所有步长1和步长2的算子。

Result: 在BraTS、MSD Hepatic Vessel和TDSC-ABUS数据集上一致提升，边界指标显著改善（BraTS Dice从86.1提升至88.9，HD95从7.1降至6.2；TDSC-ABUS HD95从39.1降至27.8），参数量从2.3M减少至0.8M。该算子还可无缝集成到nnU-Net、Swin-UNETR和MedNeXt等其他骨干网络中，无需改变其宏观架构即可获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 几何引导的采样算子有效解决了3D分割中细小结构易失真和拓扑改变的问题，在不增加（甚至大幅减少）参数的情况下显著提升边界精度，并且具有良好的一般性和可移植性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D segmentation is central to quantitative lesion assessment and anatomy mapping for clinical planning and follow-up. Thin, elongated, and fine anatomical/pathological structures (e.g., vessels) are a particularly challenging case: a one-voxel boundary error can disconnect a branch and change clinically relevant topology. In encoder-decoder networks (e.g., U-Net), repeated downsampling and fixed-grid convolution blur or alias fine structures and weaken orientation cues, so early mistakes propagate across scales. We propose a geometry-guided local operator that steers where features are sampled, rather than deforming convolutional kernels, under a single formulation for both feature refinement (stride 1) and resolution reduction (stride > 1). At each voxel, it predicts a local orientation and bounded step sizes, samples symmetrically along these directions, and transforms paired samples into compact geometric and boundary cues with lightweight mixing; a cross-scale consensus aligns encoder and decoder features at skip connections to reduce geometric mismatch. Replacing all stride 1 and stride 2 operators in a 3D U-Net yields consistent improvements on BraTS, MSD Hepatic Vessel, and TDSC-ABUS, with notably better boundary metrics (e.g., BraTS Dice 86.1 to 88.9, HD95 7.1 to 6.2; TDSC-ABUS HD95 39.1 to 27.8) while reducing parameters from 2.3M to 0.8M. We further demonstrate that the operator can be integrated into other backbones (e.g., nnU-Net, Swin-UNETR, and MedNeXt) without changing their macro-architectures while providing consistent performance gains.

</details>


### [67] [FlowMoDL: Model-Based Deep Learning with Conjugate-Gradient Data Consistency for Highly Accelerated 4D Flow MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25828)
*Tristan Gottwald,Michelle Bruch,Mubashir-Ul Hassan,Fatma Alickovic,Milan Kloiber,Daniel Tenbrinck,Torsten Panholzer,Melanie Schaller,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowMoDL，一种用于高加速4D flow MRI重建的展开神经网络，通过交替学习(3+1)D时空去噪器和基于SENSE前向模型的CG数据一致性更新，并采用双路径条件化方案和深度监督复合损失，在10x到50x加速因子下均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D flow MRI高加速重建需要在解剖幅度和相位速度精度之间取得平衡，现有方法（如MoDL、FlowVN、FlowMRI-Net）在有限梯度步数预算下性能退化严重，缺乏能够在不同加速因子下统一处理且保证生理速度准确性的重建框架。

Method: 基于MoDL框架构建FlowMoDL，交替使用学习(3+1)D时空去噪器与基于SENSE前向模型的共轭梯度数据一致性更新；引入双路径条件化方案自适应调整去噪器特征和数据一致性权重，使单一模型支持10x到50x加速；采用深度监督复合损失函数显式惩罚速度大小和角度误差，并通过课程学习策略稳定训练。

Result: 在CMRx4DFlow多中心数据集上，FlowMoDL在所有加速因子下于幅度SSIM、nRMSE、相对速度误差和角度误差方面均严格优于CG-SENSE、MoDL、FlowVN和FlowMRI-Net等基线；在有限梯度步数预算下，竞争网络的性能显著下降，而FlowMoDL能够稳健收敛并恢复锐利结构细节和时域连贯的速度场。

Conclusion: FlowMoDL通过联合优化解剖幅度和相位速度精度、双路径条件化及稳健的训练策略，在高加速4D flow MRI重建中展现出优越的梯度步高效性和全方面性能优势，为流MRI重建提供了有效的新方案。

Abstract: We present FlowMoDL, an unrolled neural network for highly accelerated 4D flow MRI reconstruction that directly optimizes for both anatomical magnitude and phase-derived velocity accuracy. Building on the MoDL framework, FlowMoDL alternates a learned (3+1)D spatiotemporal denoiser with conjugate-gradient data-consistency updates based on the SENSE forward model. A novel dual-pathway conditioning scheme adapts the denoiser features and data-consistency weighting, enabling a single model to handle varying acceleration factors ($10\times$ to $50\times$). To ensure physiological accuracy, the network is trained using a deep-supervision composite loss that explicitly penalizes velocity magnitude and angular errors, stabilized by a curriculum schedule. We evaluate FlowMoDL on the multi-center CMRx4DFlow dataset against classical and deep-learning baselines (CG-SENSE, MoDL, FlowVN, and FlowMRI-Net). A key advantage of FlowMoDL is its superior gradient step efficiency. When evaluated under an equivalent, limited budget of gradient steps, competing flow-specific networks degrade significantly. In contrast, FlowMoDL robustly converges and strictly outperforms all competitors across all acceleration factors in magnitude SSIM, nRMSE, relative velocity error, and angular error, successfully recovering sharp structural details and temporally coherent velocity fields.

</details>


### [68] [Socialized Detector Learning: Trajectory-Guided and Reciprocal Distillation for Heterogeneous Object Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25836)
*Weihao Li,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Ruipu Zhao,Boan Tao,Xinjie Yao,Yan Fan,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TGRD方法，通过轨迹引导和相互蒸馏实现异构目标检测器的社会化学习，在MS COCO上比同步聚合方法提升2.6 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测知识分散在多个异构、类别互补的检测器中，现有聚合式社会化学习未显式规划迁移顺序，而渐进式多教师蒸馏仅考虑单向学生增强，缺乏双向知识交换机制。

Method: 提出Socialized Detector Learning (SDL)框架和Trajectory-Guided and Reciprocal Distillation (TGRD)方法。TGRD通过held-out特征对齐残差估计检测器间迁移难度（IDTD），预计算分数表并贪心构建载体轨迹，沿轨迹逐步聚合知识，再通过相互迁移将知识返回各专家。

Result: 在MS COCO上使用四个异构专家和两种载体初始化，最终载体比同步聚合基线提升2.6 AP；相互蒸馏的检测器在先前不支持的类别达到20.8-28.4 AP，同时保持原有专家性能偏差在1.3 AP以内。

Conclusion: 顺序感知的渐进式知识整合加上相互迁移是检测器社会演化的可行机制，优于同步聚合方案。

Abstract: Object detection knowledge is fragmented across independently trained, heterogeneous detectors with complementary category supports. In socialized learning, this knowledge resides in a society, and learning aims to evolve the society collectively through exchange. However, aggregation-based socialization does not explicitly plan transfer order, whereas progressive multi-teacher distillation considers order but remains a one-way student enhancement in a shared category space. Building on Socialized Learning, we formulate Socialized Detector Learning (SDL) for heterogeneous, category-specialized object detectors and propose Trajectory-Guided and Reciprocal Distillation (TGRD).TGRD estimates directed operational Inter-Detector Transfer Difficulty (IDTD) from held-out feature-alignment residuals, precomputes a fixed score table, and greedily constructs a carrier trajectory. Along the trajectory, knowledge is progressively consolidated into a union-category carrier and then returned to experts through reciprocal transfer. A conditional proxy-certificate analysis shows that, under stated assumptions, the progressive certificate is no larger than an aggregated-target counterpart. On MS COCO with four heterogeneous experts and two carrier initializations, final carriers outperform epoch-matched simultaneous aggregation controls by 2.6 AP in both settings. Reciprocal detectors attain 20.8--28.4 AP on previously unsupported categories while remaining within 1.3 AP of original expert-specific performance. These results support order-aware progressive consolidation followed by reciprocal transfer as a viable mechanism for detector-society evolution.

</details>


### [69] [THA-Flow Generative Model: Prosthesis Geometry Prediction from Preoperative CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25845)
*Yiping Wang,Jie Li,Jingyu Shen,Liao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出THA-Flow，一种基于条件流匹配的生成模型，直接从术前CT生成全髋关节置换术的三维假体几何结构，首次将生成式AI应用于THA的三维手术规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全髋关节置换术的术前规划传统上被框定为选择单一假体配置和放置方案，但实际上同一解剖结构可能允许多种临床合理的解决方案，规划本质上是一个一对多的条件概率分布问题而非单一最优解问题。

Method: 使用两个AutoencoderKL分别压缩术前骨骼解剖和假体几何，用三维UNet学习从高斯噪声到假体潜空间的有向流（rectified flow），并以空间骨骼条件及可选的结构化假体参数为条件。训练数据来自1,149名患者的1,355个髋关节，通过术后CT到术前CT的刚性配准，将实际术后假体独立转换并表示为双通道截断有符号距离场。

Result: 假体自编码器在验证集上达到47.11 dB的峰值信噪比和0.9964的结构相似性指数。能够为占队列93.4%的七种主流柄型生成完整的髋臼和股骨几何。重复骨骼条件采样保持了组件位置、对齐和主要骨-假体界面，同时允许有限的局部几何变化。

Conclusion: THA-Flow是首个将生成式AI应用于THA三维手术规划的工作，证明了从术前CT直接生成多样化且临床合理的假体几何的可行性。

Abstract: Preoperative planning for total hip arthroplasty (THA) is commonly framed as selecting a single prosthesis configuration and placement for a patient's osseous anatomy. In practice, however, the same anatomy may admit several clinically reasonable solutions, making planning inherently a one-to-many problem that is better represented by a conditional probability distribution. We present THA-Flow, a conditional flow-matching model that generates three-dimensional prosthesis geometry directly from preoperative CT. Separate AutoencoderKL models compress preoperative bone anatomy and prosthesis geometry, while a three-dimensional UNet learns a rectified flow from Gaussian noise to the prosthesis latent space under spatial bone conditioning and optional structured prosthesis parameters. The retrospective cohort comprised 1,355 hips from 1,149 patients undergoing primary THA. Following rigid registration of postoperative CT to preoperative CT, the actual postoperative prostheses were transformed independently according to the pelvic and femoral registrations and represented as a dual-channel truncated signed distance field. The prosthesis autoencoder achieved a peak signal-to-noise ratio of 47.11 dB and a structural similarity index of 0.9964 on the validation set. Complete acetabular and femoral geometries were generated across seven major stem models representing 93.4% of the cohort. Repeated bone-conditioned sampling preserved component position, alignment, and the principal bone-prosthesis interfaces while allowing limited local geometric variation. To our knowledge, THA-Flow represents the first application of generative AI to three-dimensional surgical planning for THA.

</details>


### [70] [DEFUSE: Generalizable Backdoor Defense for Self-Supervised Encoders with Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25851)
*Tuo Chen,Jie Gui,Minjing Dong,Lanting Fang,Ju Jia,Benlei Cui,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEFUSE是一个针对自监督学习（SSL）编码器的通用后门检测框架，利用条件扩散生成模型将后门检测重新表述为表示条件图像似然估计问题，通过在语义重构空间中评估重建结果来检测后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSL编码器易受后门攻击，现有防御方法仅针对单一范式（视觉SSL或视觉-语言编码器），且依赖严格假设（如访问未感染分布内数据或预计算伪标签），在实际中难以满足。

Method: 提出DEFUSE框架，受贝叶斯后验推断启发，将后门检测建模为表示条件图像似然估计问题，使用条件扩散生成模型参数化该过程。由于精确似然不可解，将目标松弛为语义重构，并在参考编码器提供的良好分离表示空间中进行评估。利用预训练扩散模型进行微调而非从零训练，借助其生成先验将数据映射到自然图像流形。

Result: 大量实验表明DEFUSE在多种攻击设置下显著优于现有检测器，能同时泛化到视觉SSL和视觉-语言编码器，并且大幅减少了对受害者编码器或攻击策略先验知识的依赖。

Conclusion: DEFUSE提供了一种通用、无需严格假设的后门检测方案，能够跨范式有效检测SSL编码器中的后门攻击。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) encoders are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, posing threats to both visual SSL encoders and vision-language encoders. Existing defenses are typically designed for only one of these paradigms and rely on restrictive assumptions such as access to uninfected in-distribution data or precomputed pseudo-labels, which are difficult to satisfy in practice. To address these limitations, we propose DEFUSE, a generalizable backdoor detection framework for SSL encoders. Inspired by Bayesian posterior inference, we reformulate backdoor detection as a representation-conditioned image likelihood estimation problem parameterized by a conditional diffusion generative model. Uninfected representations tend to yield semantically consistent reconstructions, whereas backdoored ones are more likely to be mapped to the attacker's target class or semantically meaningless images, deviating from the original semantics and thereby exposing the backdoor. However, we find that the exact likelihood is intractable, because highly abstracted representations discard the low-level information necessary for pixel-faithful reconstruction. We therefore relax the objective to semantic reconstruction and evaluate it in a well-separated representation space provided by a reference encoder. Rather than training from scratch, we fine-tune a pretrained diffusion model, leveraging its generative prior to map data onto the natural image manifold while preserving semantic content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DEFUSE substantially outperforms existing detectors across diverse attack settings, generalizing to both visual SSL and vision-language encoders. Notably, our method greatly reduces the reliance on prior knowledge about the victim encoder or the attack strategy. The source code is available at https://github.com/jsrdcht/DEFUSE .

</details>


### [71] [Precipitation Downscaling Using Foundation Model-Conditioned Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25858)
*Victor Nascimento Ribeiro,Jorge Guevara,Jorge Sebastian Moraga,Chris Lucas,Natalie Lord,Andrew Taylor,Edward Lockhart,Will Trojak,Johannes Schmude,Anne Jones*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种条件化策略（拼接、交叉注意力+学习编码器、交叉注意力+预训练基础模型Prithvi WxC）对扩散模型进行降水降尺度的效果，发现交叉注意力优于简单拼接，且在数据有限时预训练基础模型表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球气候模型输出分辨率低且偏差大，难以直接用于水文影响评估。虽然基于扩散模型的AI统计降尺度前景广阔，但大尺度大气预测因子对生成过程的条件化机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）应用于日降水降尺度，比较三种条件化策略：1）上采样粗预测因子的通道拼接；2）带学习卷积编码器的交叉注意力条件化；3）使用预训练Prithvi WxC天气基础模型冻结编码器的交叉注意力条件化。所有策略与无条件基线在相同条件下评估，使用概率、分布、频谱和极端事件指标，研究区域为科罗拉多河流域。

Result: 拼接条件化在点式CRPS和MSE上最优，但产生过度平滑的场并抑制高强度事件。交叉注意力条件化在分布真实性和频谱保真度上明显更好。对极端事件改善最大：Prithvi-WxC条件化模型保留超过一半的>100mm/天事件。全数据训练时，学习卷积模型与基础模型方法表现相近但计算成本更低；然而Prithvi-WxC条件化模型仅用五年训练数据即达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力条件化比简单拼接更适合概率降水降尺度，预训练基础模型表征在数据有限场景下具有优势。

Abstract: High-resolution precipitation fields are essential for hydrological impact assessment, yet global climate model outputs are too coarse and biased for direct use. AI-based statistical downscaling with diffusion models offers a promising approach, but the mechanism by which large-scale atmospheric predictors condition generation remains largely unexplored. We investigate three conditioning strategies for a denoising diffusion probabilistic model applied to daily precipitation downscaling: channel concatenation of upsampled coarse predictors, cross-attention conditioning with a learned convolutional encoder, and cross-attention conditioning with the frozen encoder of the pretrained Prithvi WxC weather foundation model. All strategies are evaluated against an unconditioned baseline under identical conditions using probabilistic, distributional, spectral, and extreme-event metrics for the Colorado River Basin. Concatenation conditioning achieves the lowest point-wise CRPS and MSE, but tends to produce over-smoothed fields that suppress high-intensity events. In contrast, cross-attention conditioning provides substantially better distributional realism and modest improvements in spectral fidelity. Improvements are greatest for extremes: the Prithvi-WxC conditioned model retains over half of >100mm/day events, although estimates are uncertain due to limited samples. When trained on the full dataset, the learned convolutional model performs similarly to the foundation model-conditioned approach while requiring lower computational resources. However, the Prithvi-WxC-conditioned model achieves comparable performance with only five years of training data. These results indicate that cross-attention conditioning offers advantages over simple concatenation for probabilistic precipitation downscaling, and that pre-trained foundation model representations may offer benefits in data-limited settings.

</details>


### [72] [Learning Late, Guiding Early: Timestep-Decoupled Semantic Guidance for Fair Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25862)
*Subir Kumar Parida,Rajbabu Velmurugan,Ketan Kotwal,R. S. Sengar,Swati Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义边界预测器（SBP），一种推理时框架，通过在反向去噪过程中进行一次干预来实现人口统计学公平性引导，无需重新训练或微调潜在扩散模型，在CelebA-HQ上大幅提升公平性同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人脸生成中的种群失衡会传播到下游人脸识别系统，而现有公平性生成方法需要模型重训练、架构修改或反复引导，计算开销大且不灵活。

Method: SBP利用扩散时间步的语义角色解耦：从晚期潜在表示学习线性语义边界，仅在初始噪声潜在表示上应用一次干预，其余反向去噪过程保持不变。无需重训练、微调或外部平衡数据集。

Result: 在CelebA-HQ上，性别公平性差距降低98%，二元种族降低95%，四类种族降低15%，同时保持跨群体的感知图像质量，且计算开销很小。

Conclusion: SBP是一种模型无关、推理时的一次性干预框架，可轻松集成到现有预训练潜在扩散模型中，兼顾公平性与效率。

Abstract: Demographic imbalance in synthetic face generation can propagate to downstream face recognition systems, making fairness an important consideration when diffusion models are used for data generation. Existing fairness-aware generation approaches often require model retraining, architectural modifications, or repeated guidance throughout the reverse diffusion process. In this work, we introduce Semantic Boundary Predictor (SBP), an inference-time framework that performs demographic guidance through a one-shot intervention during reverse denoising. Our approach is motivated by the observation that latent representations at different diffusion timesteps play distinct semantic roles: late-stage latents provide stronger demographic separability, whereas early-stage latents offer greater flexibility for semantic intervention. SBP leverages this timestep decoupling by learning linear semantic boundaries from late-stage latent representations while applying them only once at the initial noisy latent, allowing the remainder of the reverse denoising process to proceed unchanged. The method requires neither retraining nor fine-tuning of the underlying Latent Diffusion Model and operates without external balanced datasets. Experiments on CelebA-HQ demonstrate substantial improvements in demographic fairness, reducing fairness disparity by 98% for gender, 95% for binary race, and 15% for four-class race, while maintaining perceptual image quality across demographic groups. Owing to its one-shot inference strategy and model-agnostic design, SBP introduces only a small computational overhead and can be readily integrated with existing pre-trained latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [73] [LUTSeg: A Longitudinal Multi-Expert Dataset for Ulcer Tissue Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25866)
*Karen Sanchez,Carlos Hinojosa,Albert A. Ávila,Andrea C. Riano-Rojas,Diego H. Romero,Jenny C. Páez,Martina Llinás,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LUTSeg，一个纵向慢性溃疡数据集（包含141张来自39名患者的图像，由5位临床专家标注了伤口掩膜和五种组织类别），并提出了TiSage，一个基于教师-学生架构的半监督组织分割框架，利用冻结医学视觉-语言模型的多尺度语义先验，在LUTSeg和DFUTissue上优于多数低标注设置下的监督和半监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化伤口组织成分对监测慢性溃疡进展和指导治疗决策至关重要，但像素级标注成本高昂，多组织伤口数据集稀缺，尤其是麻风病等被忽视疾病的伤口数据更为匮乏。

Method: 构建LUTSeg数据集（141张图像、39名患者、五种组织类别、多专家标注子集）；提出TiSage半监督组织分割框架，结合冻结医学视觉-语言模型的多尺度语义先验与教师-学生架构。

Result: 在LUTSeg和DFUTissue数据集上进行评估，TiSage在大多数低标注设置下优于监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUTSeg数据集和TiSage框架为慢性溃疡（尤其是麻风病等被忽视疾病）的组织分割研究提供了新的资源和有效方法，代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Quantifying wound tissue composition is essential for monitoring chronic ulcer progression and guiding treatment decisions. However, pixel-level annotations are costly, and multi-tissue wound datasets remain scarce, particularly for neglected diseases such as leprosy. We introduce LUTSeg, a longitudinal chronic ulcer dataset comprising 141 images from 39 patients with wound masks and five tissue categories annotated by five expert clinicians, including a multi-expert gold-standard subset for inter-rater agreement analysis.
